```
// @title YORO
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { Image, ScrollView } from '@tarojs/components'
import { getMainlines, getMainlineDetail } from '@/db/api'
import type { MainlineDetail } from '@/db/types'
import { countdown, prettyDate, todayStr } from '@/utils/date'
import MapCanvas from '@/components/MapCanvas'
import ScheduleView from '@/components/ScheduleView'

export default function Home() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mainlines, setMainlines] = useState<{ id: string; name: string; exam_date: string }[]>([])
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState('')
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<MainlineDetail | null>(null)
  const [view, setView] = useState<'map' | 'schedule'>('map')
  const [showGuide, setShowGuide] = useState(false)
  const mapVersion = useRef(0)

  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const lines = await getMainlines()
      if (lines.length === 0) {
        setMainlines([])
        setDetail(null)
        setShowGuide(true)
        return
      }
      setMainlines(lines.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name, exam_date: l.exam_date })))
      const target = activeId && lines.some((l) => l.id === activeId) ? activeId : lines[0].id
      setActiveId(target)
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(target)
      setDetail(d)
    } catch {
      setError('地图加载失败，请下拉重试')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeId])

  useEffect(() => { loadAll() }, [loadAll])
  useDidShow(() => { loadAll() })

  const switchLine = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    if (id === activeId) return
    setActiveId(id)
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(id)
      setDetail(d)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeId])

  // 汇总文案
  const summary = useMemo(() => {
    if (!detail) return ''
    const subjects = detail.branches.filter((b) => b.type === 'subject')
    const doneTotal = subjects.reduce((acc, b) => acc + b.nodes.filter((n) => n.status === 'done').length, 0)
    const dates = subjects.flatMap((b) => b.nodes.filter((n) => n.status === 'done' && n.done_date).map((n) => n.done_date as string))
    if (dates.length === 0) return doneTotal > 0 ? `今天做成了 ${doneTotal} 件事` : '从记一笔开始，走出第一格'
    const first = dates.slice().sort()[0]
    const days = Math.max(Math.round((Date.parse(todayStr()) - Date.parse(first)) / 86400000) + 1, 1)
    return `你已经走了 ${days} 天，${doneTotal} 格`
  }, [detail])

  const active = mainlines.find((m) => m.id === activeId)

  if (loading && mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在铺开地图…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error && mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-map-off w-16 h-16 text-muted-foreground" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">{error}</p>
        <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-6 py-3 text-xl text-primary-foreground" onClick={() => loadAll()}>
          <div className="py-1">重新加载</div>
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-map w-20 h-20 text-primary tilt-left" />
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-hand text-foreground">把你的备考画成一条路</h1>
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground text-center leading-relaxed">计划有时刻，记录无时长。先写下你的终点，或看看示例地图长什么样。</p>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 w-full">
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary shadow-sketch w-full" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>
            <div className="py-3 text-xl text-primary-foreground">写下我的终点</div>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-primary w-full" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/templates/index' })}>
            <div className="py-3 text-xl text-foreground">先随便看看示例地图</div>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col">
      {/* 首次进入引导（有数据也可从示例地图回来） */}
      {showGuide && (
        <div className="mx-4 mt-3 rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-primary shadow-sketch-sm animate-sketch-in">
          <div className="p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="i-lucide-compass w-8 h-8 text-primary shrink-0" />
            <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col gap-2">
              <p className="text-xl text-foreground">开始之前</p>
              <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">写下我的终点，或先随便看看示例地图。</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-1">
                <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-base text-primary-foreground" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>写下我的终点</button>
                <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-muted px-4 py-2 text-base text-foreground" onClick={() => { Taro.setStorageSync('yoro_guide_seen', '1'); setShowGuide(false) }}>先随便看看</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="i-lucide-x w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" onClick={() => setShowGuide(false)} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 主线切换器 */}
      <ScrollView scrollX className="w-full flex-none" enhanced showScrollbar={false}>
        <div className="flex gap-3 px-4 py-3 items-center">
          {mainlines.map((m) => {
            const cd = countdown(m.exam_date)
            const isActive = m.id === activeId
            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={m.id}
                className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 flex-none flex flex-col items-start px-4 py-2 ${isActive ? 'bg-primary border-primary shadow-sketch-sm' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`}
                onClick={() => switchLine(m.id)}
              >
                <span className={`text-xl font-hand ${isActive ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{m.name}</span>
                <span className={`text-base mt-1 ${isActive ? 'text-primary-foreground/90' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`}>{cd > 0 ? `还有 ${cd} 天` : '已到终点'}</span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 border-dashed border-border/60 flex-none px-4 py-3 bg-transparent" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>
            <div className="i-lucide-plus w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </ScrollView>

      {/* 进度一句话 */}
      <div className="px-4 pb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="i-lucide-footprints w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">{summary}</p>
      </div>

      {/* 主体视图 */}
      {view === 'map' ? (
        <div className="flex-1 relative">
          {detail ? (
            <MapCanvas
              key={`${activeId}-${mapVersion.current}`}
              detail={detail}
              onNodeClick={(nodeId) => Taro.navigateTo({ url: `/pages/node-detail/index?id=${nodeId}` })}
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-20">
              <div className="i-lucide-route w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
              <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">这条主线还没有支线，去新建一条吧</p>
            </div>
          )}
          {/* 记一笔悬浮按钮 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="absolute right-4 bottom-24 press-sketch rounded-full bg-primary shadow-sketch flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-4"
            onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: `/pages/record/index?mainline=${activeId}` })}
          >
            <div className="i-lucide-pencil-line w-6 h-6 text-primary-foreground" />
            <span className="text-xl text-primary-foreground font-hand">今天做成了什么</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ScheduleView mainlineId={activeId} onSwitchToMap={() => setView('map')} />
      )}

      {/* 底部图例 + 视图切换 */}
      <div className="flex-none border-t-2 border-dashed border-border/50 bg-background px-4 py-3">
        {view === 'map' && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-2 mb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><span className="w-5 border-t-2 border-solid border-ink inline-block" /><span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">已走</span></div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><span className="w-5 border-t-2 border-dashed border-ink inline-block" /><span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">未走</span></div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><span className="w-5 border-t-2 border-dotted border-ink inline-block" /><span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">冬眠</span></div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><span className="w-3 h-3 bg-ink rotate-45 inline-block" /><span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">补给站</span></div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><span className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 border-solid border-ink inline-block" /><span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">岔路口</span></div>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{prettyDate(todayStr())}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 border-primary bg-card px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-2"
            onClick={() => setView(view === 'map' ? 'schedule' : 'map')}
          >
            <div className={view === 'map' ? 'i-lucide-calendar-days w-5 h-5 text-primary' : 'i-lucide-map w-5 h-5 text-primary'} />
            <span className="text-xl text-primary">{view === 'map' ? '看排期' : '看地图'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Thought: 现在创建地图 Canvas 组件与排期视图组件（并行）：

Created File with Text:
```
// 手绘风格成长地图 —— Canvas 2D 绘制
// 实线=已走 / 虚线=未走 / 点线=冬眠；菱形=补给站；粗描边圆环=岔路口
// 主道节点大线粗 / 便道中等线细 / 旁路小且虚线
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import Taro from '@tarojs/taro'
import type { MainlineDetail, NodeItem } from '@/db/types'

interface Props {
  detail: MainlineDetail
  onNodeClick: (nodeId: string) => void
}

interface HitCircle {
  x: number
  y: number
  r: number
  id: string
}

const INK = '#2F4F3F'
const INK_LIGHT = 'rgba(47,79,63,0.35)'
const RED = '#FF4D4D'

// 基础 y 坐标（CSS 像素，画布宽 343，高按支线数自适应）
const TOP = 60
const LINE_GAP = 104

export default function MapCanvas({ detail, onNodeClick }: Props) {
  const canvasId = useRef(`map${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`)
  const hitsRef = useRef<HitCircle[]>([])
  const sizeRef = useRef({ w: 343, h: 400 })

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      try { draw() } catch (e) {
        console.error('map draw failed', e)
      }
    }, 80)
    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [detail])

  async function draw() {
    const query = Taro.createSelectorQuery()
    const res = await new Promise<any[]>((resolve) => {
      query.select(`#${canvasId.current}`).fields({ node: true, size: true, rect: true }).exec(resolve)
    })
    const item = res && res[0]
    if (!item || !item.node) return
    const canvas = item.node as HTMLCanvasElement
    let cssW = 343
    let cssH = 400
    if (Taro.getEnv() === 'WEAPP') {
      cssW = item.width || 343
      cssH = item.height || 400
    } else {
      const rect = (canvas as any).getBoundingClientRect?.()
      if (rect && rect.width) { cssW = rect.width; cssH = rect.height }
    }
    sizeRef.current = { w: cssW, h: cssH }
    const dpr = Taro.getSystemInfoSync().pixelRatio || 2
    canvas.width = Math.round(cssW * dpr)
    canvas.height = Math.round(cssH * dpr)
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
    if (!ctx) return
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0)
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cssW, cssH)
    const hits: HitCircle[] = []

    const subjects = detail.branches.filter((b) => b.type === 'subject')
    if (subjects.length === 0) return

    // ---------- 主线（顶部粗横线 + 终点旗） ----------
    const mainY = 34
    ctx.save()
    ctx.strokeStyle = INK
    ctx.lineWidth = 3
    ctx.setLineDash([])
    ctx.beginPath()
    ctx.moveTo(16, mainY)
    ctx.lineTo(cssW - 44, mainY)
    ctx.stroke()
    // 终点小旗
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5
    ctx.beginPath()
    ctx.moveTo(cssW - 44, mainY - 16)
    ctx.lineTo(cssW - 44, mainY + 6)
    ctx.stroke()
    ctx.fillStyle = RED
    ctx.beginPath()
    ctx.moveTo(cssW - 44, mainY - 16)
    ctx.lineTo(cssW - 24, mainY - 11)
    ctx.lineTo(cssW - 44, mainY - 6)
    ctx.closePath()
    ctx.fill()
    ctx.fillStyle = INK
    ctx.font = '600 13px sans-serif'
    ctx.fillText('终点', cssW - 60, mainY + 24)
    ctx.font = '600 15px sans-serif'
    ctx.fillText(detail.name, 16, mainY - 12)
    ctx.restore()

    // ---------- 支线 ----------
    subjects.forEach((branch, bi) => {
      const y = TOP + bi * LINE_GAP
      const hibernate = branch.status === 'hibernate'
      const doneNodes = branch.nodes.filter((n) => n.status === 'done')
      const plannedNodes = branch.nodes.filter((n) => n.status === 'planned')
      const allNodes = [...doneNodes, ...plannedNodes]
      if (allNodes.length === 0) {
        ctx.save()
        ctx.fillStyle = INK
        ctx.font = '600 15px sans-serif'
        ctx.fillText(branch.name, 16, y - 18)
        ctx.strokeStyle = INK_LIGHT
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5
        ctx.setLineDash([2, 5])
        ctx.beginPath()
        ctx.moveTo(16, y)
        ctx.lineTo(cssW - 16, y)
        ctx.stroke()
        ctx.restore()
        return
      }

      // 节点横向布局：等分，节点大小按分量
      const padX = 26
      const usable = cssW - padX * 2
      const step = allNodes.length > 1 ? usable / (allNodes.length - 1) : 0
      const xs = allNodes.map((_, i) => (allNodes.length > 1 ? padX + i * step : cssW / 2))

      // 支线名称
      ctx.save()
      ctx.fillStyle = hibernate ? INK_LIGHT : INK
      ctx.font = '600 15px sans-serif'
      ctx.fillText(`${branch.name}${hibernate ? ' · 冬眠中' : ''}`, 16, y - 24)
      ctx.restore()

      // 逐段画线
      for (let i = 0; i < allNodes.length - 1; i++) {
        const leftDone = allNodes[i].status === 'done'
        ctx.save()
        ctx.strokeStyle = hibernate ? INK_LIGHT : leftDone ? INK : INK_LIGHT
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.2
        if (hibernate) ctx.setLineDash([1.5, 6])
        else if (!leftDone) ctx.setLineDash([7, 5])
        else ctx.setLineDash([])
        ctx.beginPath()
        ctx.moveTo(xs[i], y)
        ctx.lineTo(xs[i + 1], y)
        ctx.stroke()
        ctx.restore()
      }

      // 画节点
      allNodes.forEach((node, i) => {
        drawNode(ctx, xs[i], y, node, hibernate, hits)
        // 标注：完成内容（done）或节点名（planned）
        ctx.save()
        const label = node.status === 'done' ? (node.done_content || node.name) : node.name
        ctx.fillStyle = hibernate ? INK_LIGHT : node.status === 'done' ? INK : INK_LIGHT
        ctx.font = '12px sans-serif'
        const text = label.length > 7 ? `${label.slice(0, 7)}…` : label
        const tx = Math.min(Math.max(xs[i] - 30, 8), cssW - 76)
        const ty = node.weight === 'bypass' ? y + 26 : y + 22
        ctx.fillText(text, tx, ty)
        if (node.mood) {
          // 情绪痕迹：小圆点
          ctx.fillStyle = RED
          ctx.beginPath()
          ctx.arc(tx + ctx.measureText(text).width + 6, ty - 4, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2)
          ctx.fill()
        }
        ctx.restore()
      })
    })

    hitsRef.current = hits
  }

  function drawNode(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, node: NodeItem, hibernate: boolean, hits: HitCircle[]) {
    const done = node.status === 'done'
    const supply = node.kind === 'supply'
    const crossing = node.is_crossing
    let r = 7
    if (node.weight === 'main') r = 9
    else if (node.weight === 'side') r = 6.5
    else r = 5
    if (supply) r = 8

    ctx.save()
    ctx.strokeStyle = hibernate ? INK_LIGHT : INK
    ctx.fillStyle = done && !hibernate ? INK : '#FDFBF7'

    if (supply) {
      // 菱形补给站
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.4
      ctx.beginPath()
      ctx.moveTo(x, y - r)
      ctx.lineTo(x + r, y)
      ctx.lineTo(x, y + r)
      ctx.lineTo(x - r, y)
      ctx.closePath()
      if (done && !hibernate) ctx.fill()
      ctx.stroke()
    } else if (crossing) {
      // 粗描边圆环（岔路口）
      ctx.lineWidth = 3.5
      ctx.beginPath()
      ctx.arc(x, y, r + 2, 0, Math.PI * 2)
      ctx.stroke()
      if (done && !hibernate) {
        ctx.fillStyle = INK
        ctx.beginPath()
        ctx.arc(x, y, r - 2, 0, Math.PI * 2)
        ctx.fill()
      }
      hits.push({ x, y, r: r + 12, id: node.id })
      ctx.restore()
      return
    } else {
      // 常规节点；旁路用虚线描边
      ctx.lineWidth = node.weight === 'main' ? 3 : node.weight === 'side' ? 2 : 1.5
      if (node.weight === 'bypass') ctx.setLineDash([2.5, 2.5])
      ctx.beginPath()
      ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2)
      if (done && !hibernate) ctx.fill()
      ctx.stroke()
    }
    hits.push({ x, y, r: r + 11, id: node.id })
    ctx.restore()
  }

  function handleTap(e: any) => {
    const touch = e.changedTouches?.[0] ?? e.touches?.[0]
    if (!touch) return
    let px = touch.x
    let py = touch.y
    if (px == null || py == null) {
      // H5: 用 client 坐标减画布偏移
      const query = Taro.createSelectorQuery()
      query.select(`#${canvasId.current}`).boundingClientRect((rect: any) => {
        if (rect) tryHit(touch.clientX - rect.left, touch.clientY - rect.top)
      }).exec()
      return
    }
    tryHit(px, py)
  }

  function tryHit(x: number, y: number) {
    for (const h of hitsRef.current) {
      const dx = x - h.x
      const dy = y - h.y
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= h.r * h.r) {
        onNodeClick(h.id)
        return
      }
    }
  }

  const subjects = detail.branches.filter((b) => b.type === 'subject')
  const height = Math.max(TOP + subjects.length * LINE_GAP, 360)

  return (
    <div className="w-full px-4 pb-4">
      <canvas
        id={canvasId.current}
        type="2d"
        style={{ width: '100%', height: `${height}px` }}
        className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 shadow-sketch-sm"
        onTouchEnd={handleTap as any}
      />
    </div>
  )
}
```

Created File with Text:
```
// 排期视图 —— 日/周视图 + 时间网格 + 拖动改计划（仅改计划，不产生完成记录）
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro from '@tarojs/taro'
import { ScrollView } from '@tarojs/components'
import { getMainlineDetail, updatePlan } from '@/db/api'
import type { MainlineDetail, NodeItem } from '@/db/types'
import { addDays, fmt, minutesToHM, parseDate, timeToMinutes, todayStr, weekStart, weekdayCN } from '@/utils/date'

interface Props {
  mainlineId: string
  onSwitchToMap: () => void
}

const HOUR_START = 7
const HOUR_END = 23
const ROWS = HOUR_END - HOUR_START
const ROW_H = 52
const HOUR_LABEL_W = 44
const DAY_W = 42
const GRID_TOP = 34

function nodeColor(n: NodeItem): string {
  if (n.weight === 'main') return 'bg-primary'
  if (n.weight === 'side') return 'bg-primary/60'
  return 'bg-accent/70'
}

export default function ScheduleView({ mainlineId }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'day' | 'week'>('week')
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<MainlineDetail | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [curDate, setCurDate] = useState(todayStr())
  const [dragId, setDragId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [dragOffsets, setDragOffsets] = useState<Record<string, { day: number; startMin: number; endMin: number }>>({})

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!mainlineId) return
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(mainlineId)
      setDetail(d)
    } catch {
      setError('排期加载失败')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [mainlineId])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])

  const ws = weekStart(curDate)
  const weekDays = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => addDays(ws, i)), [ws])

  // 当前主线全部有计划时段的节点
  const plannedNodes = useMemo(() => {
    if (!detail) return []
    return detail.branches
      .filter((b) => b.type === 'subject' && b.status !== 'archived')
      .flatMap((b) => b.nodes.filter((n) => n.plan_date && n.plan_start && n.plan_end))
  }, [detail])

  function dayNodes(date: string): NodeItem[] {
    return plannedNodes.filter((n) => (dragOffsets[n.id] ? addDays(n.plan_date as string, dragOffsets[n.id].day) === date : n.plan_date === date))
  }

  function offsetOf(n: NodeItem) {
    return dragOffsets[n.id] ?? { day: 0, startMin: timeToMinutes(n.plan_start as string), endMin: timeToMinutes(n.plan_end as string) }
  }

  function topOf(n: NodeItem): number {
    const o = offsetOf(n)
    return GRID_TOP + ((o.startMin / 60 - HOUR_START) * ROW_H)
  }

  function heightOf(n: NodeItem): number {
    const o = offsetOf(n)
    return Math.max(((o.endMin - o.startMin) / 60) * ROW_H, 26)
  }

  // ---------- 拖动逻辑（触摸） ----------
  function onNodeDragStart(e: any, n: NodeItem, dayIdx: number) {
    const touch = e.changedTouches?.[0] ?? e.touches?.[0]
    if (!touch) return
    const o = offsetOf(n)
    const ctx = { day: dayIdx, startY: touch.clientY, startMin: o.startMin, endMin: o.endMin, mode: 'move' as const }
    ;(e.currentTarget as any).__drag = ctx
    setDragId(n.id)
  }

  function onNodeDragMove(e: any, n: NodeItem) {
    const touch = e.changedTouches?.[0] ?? e.touches?.[0]
    const el = e.currentTarget as any
    const ctx = el.__drag
    if (!touch || !ctx) return
    const dy = touch.clientY - ctx.startY
    const minDelta = Math.round((dy / ROW_H) * 60 / 15) * 15
    const dayDelta = ctx.mode === 'move' ? 0 : 0
    const o = dragOffsets[n.id] ?? { day: 0, startMin: ctx.startMin, endMin: ctx.endMin }
    setDragOffsets((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [n.id]: { day: o.day + dayDelta, startMin: clampMin(ctx.startMin + minDelta), endMin: clampMin(ctx.endMin + minDelta) },
    }))
  }

  function clampMin(m: number): number {
    return Math.min(Math.max(m, HOUR_START * 60), HOUR_END * 60)
  }

  async function onNodeDragEnd(e: any, n: NodeItem) {
    const el = e.currentTarget as any
    const ctx = el.__drag
    setDragId(null)
    if (!ctx) { delete el.__drag; return }
    delete el.__drag
    const o = dragOffsets[n.id]
    if (!o) return
    const base = n.plan_date as string
    const newDate = addDays(base, o.day)
    const startMin = o.startMin
    const endMin = Math.max(o.endMin, startMin + 30)
    const payload = { plan_date: newDate, plan_start: minutesToHM(startMin), plan_end: minutesToHM(endMin) }
    try {
      await updatePlan(n.id, payload)
      Taro.showToast({ title: '计划已更新', icon: 'none' })
      await load()
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '保存失败，请重试', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  // ---------- 布局 ----------
  const gridW = mode === 'week' ? HOUR_LABEL_W + DAY_W * 7 : HOUR_LABEL_W + 300

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-20">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在展开排期…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-20 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-calendar-off w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">{error}</p>
        <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-6 py-2" onClick={() => load()}>
          <div className="py-1 text-xl text-primary-foreground">重试</div>
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
      {/* 固定说明 */}
      <div className="mx-4 mt-2 rounded-sketch bg-muted px-4 py-2 border-2 border-border/30">
        <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">这里排的是打算什么时候走；走完了回地图记一笔，不看用了多久。</p>
      </div>

      {/* 视图切换 + 日期切换 */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 gap-2">
        <div className="flex rounded-sketch border-2 border-primary overflow-hidden">
          <button type="button" className={`px-4 py-1 ${mode === 'day' ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-card'}`} onClick={() => setMode('day')}>
            <span className={`text-lg ${mode === 'day' ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>日</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className={`px-4 py-1 ${mode === 'week' ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-card'}`} onClick={() => setMode('week')}>
            <span className={`text-lg ${mode === 'week' ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>周</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/40 w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center" onClick={() => setCurDate(mode === 'week' ? addDays(ws, -7) : addDays(curDate, -1))}>
            <div className="i-lucide-chevron-left w-5 h-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
          <span className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand min-w-24 text-center">{mode === 'week' ? `${fmt(ws).slice(5)} 那周` : fmt(curDate).slice(5)}</span>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/40 w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center" onClick={() => setCurDate(mode === 'week' ? addDays(ws, 7) : addDays(curDate, 1))}>
            <div className="i-lucide-chevron-right w-5 h-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 时间网格 */}
      <ScrollView scrollX className="flex-1" enhanced showScrollbar={false}>
        <div style={{ width: `${gridW}px` }} className="relative">
          {/* 小时行 */}
          {Array.from({ length: ROWS }, (_, i) => {
            const h = HOUR_START + i
            return (
              <div key={h} className="absolute left-0 right-0 border-t border-dashed border-border/30" style={{ top: `${GRID_TOP + i * ROW_H}px`, height: `${ROW_H}px` }}>
                <span className="absolute -top-2 left-1 text-base text-muted-foreground bg-background px-1">{h}:00</span>
              </div>
            )
          })}
          {/* 周表头 */}
          {mode === 'week' ? (
            weekDays.map((d, i) => (
              <div key={d} className={`absolute text-center ${d === todayStr() ? 'text-primary font-bold' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`} style={{ left: `${HOUR_LABEL_W + i * DAY_W}px`, top: '4px', width: `${DAY_W}px` }}>
                <p className="text-base leading-tight">周{weekdayCN(d)}</p>
                <p className="text-base leading-tight">{parseDate(d).getDate()}</p>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <div className="absolute text-center text-muted-foreground" style={{ left: `${HOUR_LABEL_W}px`, top: '4px', width: '300px' }}>
              <p className="text-base">{fmt(curDate)} 周{weekdayCN(curDate)}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* 节点块 */}
          {(mode === 'week' ? weekDays : [curDate]).map((d, di) =>
            dayNodes(d).map((n) => (
              <div
                key={n.id}
                className={`absolute rounded-sketch-sm border-2 ${nodeColor(n)} ${dragId === n.id ? 'opacity-80 border-accent' : 'border-ink/50'} overflow-hidden`}
                style={{
                  left: `${mode === 'week' ? HOUR_LABEL_W + di * DAY_W + 3 : HOUR_LABEL_W + 8}px`,
                  width: mode === 'week' ? `${DAY_W - 6}px` : '284px',
                  top: `${topOf(n)}px`,
                  height: `${heightOf(n)}px`,
                  zIndex: dragId === n.id ? 20 : 10,
                  transform: dragId === n.id ? 'scale(1.03)' : 'scale(1)',
                  transition: 'transform 0.12s ease',
                }}
                onTouchStart={(e) => onNodeDragStart(e, n, mode === 'week' ? di : 0)}
                onTouchMove={(e) => onNodeDragMove(e, n)}
                onTouchEnd={(e) => onNodeDragEnd(e, n)}
                onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: `/pages/node-detail/index?id=${n.id}` })}
              >
                <div className="px-1 py-0.5 leading-tight">
                  <p className="text-base text-white truncate">{n.name}</p>
                  {heightOf(n) > 44 && <p className="text-sm text-white/80 truncate">{n.plan_start?.slice(0, 5)}-{n.plan_end?.slice(0, 5)}</p>}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}

          {/* 网格竖线 */}
          {mode === 'week'
            ? weekDays.map((d, i) => (
                <div key={`line-${d}`} className="absolute top-8 bottom-0 border-l border-dashed border-border/30" style={{ left: `${HOUR_LABEL_W + i * DAY_W}px` }} />
              ))
            : (
              <div className="absolute top-8 bottom-0 border-l border-dashed border-border/30" style={{ left: `${HOUR_LABEL_W}px` }} />
            )}
          <div style={{ height: `${GRID_TOP + ROWS * ROW_H}px` }} />
        </div>
      </ScrollView>

      {plannedNodes.length === 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 py-8">
          <div className="i-lucide-calendar-plus w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground" />
          <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">还没有计划节点，先在地图上建几格</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

```

```
// @title 记一笔
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { getMainlineDetail, recordDone } from '@/db/api'
import type { MainlineDetail } from '@/db/types'
import { QUICK_TAGS, WEIGHTS } from '@/db/types'
import { prettyDate, todayStr } from '@/utils/date'

export default function Record() {
  const [mainlineId, setMainlineId] = useState('')
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<MainlineDetail | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [branchId, setBranchId] = useState('')
  const [content, setContent] = useState('')
  const [weight, setWeight] = useState<'main' | 'side' | 'bypass'>('main')
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<{ branch?: string; content?: string }>({})

  useEffect(() => {
    const params = Taro.getCurrentInstance().router?.params || {}
    const ml = params.mainline || ''
    const br = params.branch || ''
    setMainlineId(ml)
    setBranchId(br)
  }, [])

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!mainlineId) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(mainlineId)
      setDetail(d)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [mainlineId])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
  useDidShow(() => { load() })

  const branches = useMemo(() => detail?.branches.filter((b) => b.status !== 'archived') ?? [], [detail])

  async function handleSave() {
    const errs: typeof errors = {}
    if (!branchId) errs.branch = '选一条支线再记'
    if (!content.trim()) errs.content = '写下今天做成了什么'
    setErrors(errs)
    if (Object.keys(errs).length > 0) {
      Taro.showToast({ title: Object.values(errs)[0], icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    const branch = branches.find((b) => b.id === branchId)
    if (!branch) return
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await recordDone(branch, content.trim(), weight)
      Taro.showToast({ title: '又走了一格', icon: 'success' })
      setTimeout(() => Taro.navigateBack(), 600)
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '保存失败，请重试', icon: 'none' })
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在取支线…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!detail) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-map-off w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">找不到这条主线</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col px-4 py-4 gap-5">
      {/* 日期 */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="i-lucide-calendar w-6 h-6 text-primary" />
        <p className="text-2xl font-hand text-foreground">{prettyDate(todayStr())}</p>
      </div>

      {/* 支线选择 */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground">记到哪条支线</p>
        {errors.branch && <p className="text-base text-destructive">{errors.branch}</p>}
        {branches.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-dashed border-border/50 p-4 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">这条主线还没有支线</p>
            <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-4 py-2" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>
              <div className="text-lg text-primary-foreground">去新建主线</div>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
            {branches.map((b) => {
              const selected = b.id === branchId
              const hibernate = b.status === 'hibernate'
              return (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  key={b.id}
                  className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-1 ${selected ? 'bg-primary border-primary shadow-sketch-sm' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`}
                  onClick={() => setBranchId(b.id)}
                >
                  {b.type === 'habit' && <div className={`i-lucide-footprints w-5 h-5 ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`} />}
                  <span className={`text-xl ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : hibernate ? 'text-muted-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{b.name}</span>
                  {hibernate && !selected && <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">·冬眠</span>}
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 完成内容 */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground">今天做成了什么</p>
        {errors.content && <p className="text-base text-destructive">{errors.content}</p>}
        <div className="rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-card px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
          <textarea
            className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
            style={{ height: '20vh' }}
            placeholder="例如：高数第三章过完，课后题全对"
            value={content}
            onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setContent(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
          {QUICK_TAGS.map((t) => (
            <button key={t} type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-border/30 px-3 py-1" onClick={() => setContent((c) => (c ? `${c} ${t}` : t))}>
              <span className="text-lg text-foreground">{t}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 分量标记 */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground">这一笔的分量</p>
        <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">只影响节点在地图上的大小与线型</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {WEIGHTS.map((w) => {
            const selected = w.key === weight
            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={w.key}
                className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-2 ${selected ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`}
                onClick={() => setWeight(w.key)}
              >
                <span className={`text-xl ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{w.label}</span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1" />

      {/* 保存 */}
      <button
        type="button"
        className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch w-full shadow-sketch ${saving ? 'bg-primary/50' : 'bg-primary'}`}
        onClick={handleSave}
      >
        <div className="py-3 text-2xl text-primary-foreground font-hand">{saving ? '正在记录…' : '保存这一笔'}</div>
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
// @title 节点详情
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro from '@tarojs/taro'
import { getNodeWithBranch, getSubsteps, addSubstep, toggleSubstep, updateNode, setBranchStatus, deleteSubstep } from '@/db/api'
import type { Branch, NodeItem, Substep } from '@/db/types'
import { MOODS, WEIGHTS } from '@/db/types'
import { prettyDate } from '@/utils/date'

export default function NodeDetail() {
  const [nodeId, setNodeId] = useState('')
  const [node, setNode] = useState<NodeItem | null>(null)
  const [branch, setBranch] = useState<Branch | null>(null)
  const [substeps, setSubsteps] = useState<Substep[]>([])
  const [newStep, setNewStep] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [mood, setMood] = useState('')
  const [moodNote, setMoodNote] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    const params = Taro.getCurrentInstance().router?.params || {}
    setNodeId(params.id || '')
  }, [])

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!nodeId) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const res = await getNodeWithBranch(nodeId)
      if (res) {
        setNode(res.node)
        setBranch(res.branch)
        setMood(res.node.mood || '')
        setMoodNote(res.node.mood_note || '')
        setSubsteps(await getSubsteps(nodeId))
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [nodeId])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])

  const hibernate = useMemo(() => branch?.status === 'hibernate', [branch])

  async function handleAddStep() {
    if (!newStep.trim() || !nodeId) return
    try {
      await addSubstep(nodeId, newStep.trim())
      setNewStep('')
      setSubsteps(await getSubsteps(nodeId))
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '添加失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleToggle(step: Substep) {
    try {
      await toggleSubstep(step.id, !step.checked)
      setSubsteps(await getSubsteps(nodeId))
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleDeleteStep(step: Substep) {
    try {
      await deleteSubstep(step.id)
      setSubsteps(await getSubsteps(nodeId))
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '删除失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleWeight(w: 'main' | 'side' | 'bypass') {
    if (!node) return
    try {
      await updateNode(node.id, { weight: w })
      setNode({ ...node, weight: w })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '修改失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleSaveMood() {
    if (!node || !mood) {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '先选一个情绪', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await updateNode(node.id, { mood, mood_note: moodNote.trim() || null })
      Taro.showToast({ title: '已留下情绪痕迹', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '保存失败', icon: 'none' })
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleSupply() {
    if (!node) return
    const kind = node.kind === 'supply' ? 'normal' : 'supply'
    try {
      await updateNode(node.id, { kind })
      setNode({ ...node, kind })
      Taro.showToast({ title: kind === 'supply' ? '已生成补给站' : '已移除补给站', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleHibernate() {
    if (!branch || !node) return
    const target = hibernate ? 'active' : 'hibernate'
    try {
      await setBranchStatus(branch.id, target)
      setBranch({ ...branch, status: target })
      Taro.showToast({ title: target === 'hibernate' ? '这条支线已冬眠' : '这条支线已唤醒', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在打开节点…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!node || !branch) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-circle-slash w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">找不到这个节点</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col px-4 py-4 gap-5">
      {/* 头部 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 shadow-sketch-sm p-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border border-border/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-muted-foreground">{branch.name}</span>
          {node.is_crossing && <span className="rounded-sketch bg-primary px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-primary-foreground">岔路口</span>}
          {node.kind === 'supply' && <span className="rounded-sketch bg-accent/80 px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-accent-foreground">补给站</span>}
          {hibernate && <span className="rounded-sketch bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-muted-foreground">冬眠中</span>}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-hand text-foreground">{node.name}</h1>
        {node.done_content && <p className="text-xl text-foreground leading-relaxed">「{node.done_content}」</p>}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 mt-1">
          {node.done_date && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <div className="i-lucide-check w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">完成于 {prettyDate(node.done_date)}</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {node.plan_date && node.plan_start && node.plan_end && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <div className="i-lucide-clock w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">计划 {prettyDate(node.plan_date)} {node.plan_start.slice(0, 5)}-{node.plan_end.slice(0, 5)}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 子步骤 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">子步骤</p>
        {substeps.length === 0 && <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">还没有子步骤，加一个拆小它</p>}
        {substeps.map((s) => (
          <div key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button type="button" className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-sketch-sm border-2 flex items-center justify-center flex-none ${s.checked ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card border-input'}`} onClick={() => handleToggle(s)}>
              {s.checked && <div className="i-lucide-check w-5 h-5 text-primary-foreground" />}
            </button>
            <span className={`flex-1 text-xl ${s.checked ? 'text-muted-foreground line-through' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{s.content}</span>
            <button type="button" className="i-lucide-trash-2 w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground flex-none" onClick={() => handleDeleteStep(s)} />
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <div className="flex-1 rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-card px-3 py-2 overflow-hidden">
            <input
              className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
              placeholder="新增子步骤"
              value={newStep}
              onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setNewStep(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
            />
          </div>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-4" onClick={handleAddStep}>
            <div className="text-xl text-primary-foreground">加</div>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 分量 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">分量</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {WEIGHTS.map((w) => {
            const selected = w.key === node.weight
            return (
              <button key={w.key} type="button" className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-2 ${selected ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`} onClick={() => handleWeight(w.key)}>
                <span className={`text-xl ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{w.label}</span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 情绪与状态 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">此刻状态</p>
        {node.mood && !saving && (
          <div className="rounded-sketch bg-muted p-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className={`${MOODS.find((m) => m.key === node.mood)?.icon ?? 'i-lucide-feather'} w-5 h-5 text-primary`} />
            <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">已留下：{MOODS.find((m) => m.key === node.mood)?.label}{node.mood_note ? ` · ${node.mood_note}` : ''}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {MOODS.map((m) => {
            const selected = m.key === mood
            return (
              <button key={m.key} type="button" className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-2 flex flex-col items-center gap-1 ${selected ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`} onClick={() => setMood(m.key)}>
                <div className={`${m.icon} w-6 h-6 ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`} />
                <span className={`text-lg ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{m.label}</span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-card px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
          <input
            className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
            placeholder="一句话写写此刻的状态"
            value={moodNote}
            onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setMoodNote(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
          />
        </div>
        <button type="button" className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch ${saving ? 'bg-primary/50' : 'bg-primary'}`} onClick={handleSaveMood}>
          <div className="py-2 text-xl text-primary-foreground">保存状态</div>
        </button>
        <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">保存后会在地图的节点上留下痕迹，点开随时回看。</p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1" />

      {/* 岔路口操作区 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-dashed border-border/50 p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">岔路口</p>
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button type="button" className="flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-primary" onClick={handleSupply}>
            <div className="py-2 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <div className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${node.kind === 'supply' ? 'bg-primary' : 'border-2 border-primary'} rotate-45`} />
              <span className="text-lg text-foreground">{node.kind === 'supply' ? '撤掉补给站' : '休整一下'}</span>
            </div>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-primary" onClick={handleHibernate}>
            <div className="py-2 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <div className={`${hibernate ? 'i-lucide-sun' : 'i-lucide-moon'} w-6 h-6 text-primary`} />
              <span className="text-lg text-foreground">{hibernate ? '唤醒这条支线' : '让这条支线冬眠'}</span>
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>
        {!hibernate && <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">冬眠的支线会变成点线，不会有任何催促，随时唤醒。</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

```

Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
// @title 今日日记
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { Image } from '@tarojs/components'
import { addDiary, getAllDoneNodes, getDiaries } from '@/db/api'
import type { Diary } from '@/db/types'
import { MOODS } from '@/db/types'
import { prettyDate, todayStr } from '@/utils/date'
import { selectMediaFiles, uploadToSupabase } from '@/utils/upload'
import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase'

const BUCKET = 'yoro_images'

export default function DiaryPage() {
  const [diaries, setDiaries] = useState<Diary[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [content, setContent] = useState('')
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState('')
  const [mood, setMood] = useState('')
  // 待上传图片：weapp 用 tempFilePath 预览，H5 用 File
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ path: string; file: any }[]>([])
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [todayDone, setTodayDone] = useState<{ name: string; content: string | null }[]>([])

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      setDiaries(await getDiaries())
      const nodes = await getAllDoneNodes()
      setTodayDone(nodes.filter((n) => n.done_date === todayStr()).slice(0, 8).map((n) => ({ name: n.name, content: n.done_content })))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
  useDidShow(() => { load() })

  async function handlePickImage() {
    try {
      const files = await selectMediaFiles({ mediaType: ['image'], count: 9 - pending.length })
      const list = Array.isArray(files) ? files : []
      if (list.length === 0) return
      setPending((p) => [...p, ...list.map((f: any) => ({ path: (f as any).tempFilePath ?? (f as any).path ?? '', file: f }))])
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '选择图片失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  function removePending(i: number) {
    setPending((p) => p.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))
  }

  async function uploadAll(): Promise<string[]> {
    const urls: string[] = []
    for (const item of pending) {
      const res = await uploadToSupabase(item.file, { bucket: BUCKET, userId: 'diary' })
      if (!res.success || !res.data) throw new Error(res.error || '上传失败')
      const { data } = supabase.storage.from(BUCKET).getPublicUrl(res.data.path)
      urls.push(data.publicUrl)
    }
    return urls
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!content.trim()) {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '写点什么再保存吧', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    setSaving(true)
    setUploading(pending.length > 0)
    try {
      const images = await uploadAll()
      await addDiary({ content: content.trim(), images, tags, mood: mood || null })
      setContent('')
      setTags([])
      setMood('')
      setPending([])
      Taro.showToast({ title: '日记已收好', icon: 'success' })
      await load()
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '保存失败，请重试', icon: 'none' })
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
      setUploading(false)
    }
  }

  function handleAddTag() {
    const t = tagInput.trim()
    if (!t) return
    if (!tags.includes(t)) setTags((prev) => [...prev, t])
    setTagInput('')
  }

  const grouped = useMemo(() => diaries, [diaries])

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col px-4 py-4 gap-5">
      {/* 日期 */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="i-lucide-notebook-pen w-6 h-6 text-primary" />
        <p className="text-2xl font-hand text-foreground">{prettyDate(todayStr())}</p>
      </div>

      {/* 今天走过的节点 */}
      {todayDone.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-3 flex flex-col gap-1">
          <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">今天走过的格子</p>
          <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
            {todayDone.map((n, i) => (
              <span key={i} className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border border-border/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-foreground">{n.content || n.name}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 编辑区 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 shadow-sketch-sm p-4 flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-background px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
          <textarea
            className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
            style={{ height: '18vh' }}
            placeholder="今天走过的路，写下来给以后的自己看"
            value={content}
            onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setContent(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
          />
        </div>

        {/* 图片 */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <p className="text-xl text-foreground">图片</p>
          <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
            {pending.map((p, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="relative w-20 h-20 rounded-sketch-sm border-2 border-border/40 overflow-hidden">
                <Image src={p.path} mode="aspectFill" className="w-full h-full" />
                <button type="button" className="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-background/80 rounded-bl-lg w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center" onClick={() => removePending(i)}>
                  <div className="i-lucide-x w-4 h-4 text-foreground" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
            {pending.length < 9 && (
              <button type="button" className="press-sketch w-20 h-20 rounded-sketch-sm border-2 border-dashed border-input bg-muted flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1" onClick={handlePickImage}>
                <div className="i-lucide-image-plus w-7 h-7 text-muted-foreground" />
                <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">加图</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 标签 */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <p className="text-xl text-foreground">标签</p>
          <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
            {tags.map((t) => (
              <span key={t} className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border border-border/40 px-3 py-1 text-lg text-foreground flex items-center gap-1">
                {t}
                <button type="button" className="i-lucide-x w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" onClick={() => setTags((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x !== t))} />
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <div className="flex-1 rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-background px-3 py-2 overflow-hidden">
              <input
                className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
                placeholder="加一个标签，回车确认"
                value={tagInput}
                onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setTagInput(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
                onConfirm={handleAddTag}
                confirmType="done"
              />
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-border/40 px-4" onClick={handleAddTag}>
              <div className="text-xl text-foreground">加</div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 情绪 */}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {MOODS.map((m) => {
            const selected = m.key === mood
            return (
              <button key={m.key} type="button" className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-1.5 flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 ${selected ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-background border-border/40'}`} onClick={() => setMood(selected ? '' : m.key)}>
                <div className={`${m.icon} w-5 h-5 ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`} />
                <span className={`text-base ${selected ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{m.label}</span>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        <button type="button" className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch ${saving ? 'bg-primary/50' : 'bg-primary'}`} onClick={handleSave}>
          <div className="py-2.5 text-xl text-primary-foreground font-hand">{uploading ? '图片上传中…' : saving ? '正在保存…' : '收好这篇日记'}</div>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 历史日记 */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 pb-6">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">过去的日记</p>
        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8 gap-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
            <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">翻日记中…</p>
          </div>
        ) : grouped.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-dashed border-border/50 p-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div className="i-lucide-notebook-pen w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground" />
            <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">还没有日记，今天写第一篇</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          grouped.map((d) => {
            const isOpen = expanded === d.id
            const moodInfo = MOODS.find((m) => m.key === d.mood)
            return (
              <div key={d.id} className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-4 flex flex-col gap-2 press-sketch" onClick={() => setExpanded(isOpen ? null : d.id)}>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-lg text-muted-foreground">{prettyDate(d.diary_date)}</span>
                  {moodInfo && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <div className={`${moodInfo.icon} w-4 h-4 text-primary`} />
                      <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{moodInfo.label}</span>
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className={`text-xl text-foreground leading-relaxed ${isOpen ? '' : 'overflow-hidden line-clamp-2'}`}>{d.content}</p>
                {d.images.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2 mt-1">
                    {d.images.slice(0, isOpen ? 9 : 3).map((img, i) => (
                      <Image key={i} src={img} mode="aspectFill" className="w-20 h-20 rounded-sketch-sm border-2 border-border/40" />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
                {d.tags.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {d.tags.map((t) => (
                      <span key={t} className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border border-border/40 px-2 py-0.5 text-base text-muted-foreground">#{t}</span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

**FileEditAction**
Path: [/workspace/app-fe8g6wiaw16p/src/pages/habits/index.tsx]
Thought: 
Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
// @title 习惯支线
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { addFootprint, createBranches, archiveBranch, getFootprints, getHabitBranches, updateBranchRule } from '@/db/api'
import type { Branch } from '@/db/types'
import { todayStr } from '@/utils/date'

export default function Habits() {
  const [habits, setHabits] = useState<Branch[]>([])
  const [footMap, setFootMap] = useState<Record<string, string[]>>({})
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('')
  const [newRule, setNewRule] = useState<'daily' | 'weekly'>('daily')
  const [editId, setEditId] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const hs = await getHabitBranches()
      setHabits(hs)
      const fps = await getFootprints(hs.map((h) => h.id))
      const map: Record<string, string[]> = {}
      fps.forEach((f) => {
        map[f.branch_id] = [...(map[f.branch_id] ?? []), f.footprint_date]
      })
      setFootMap(map)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
  useDidShow(() => { load() })

  const today = todayStr()

  async function handleCheckin(h: Branch) {
    try {
      await addFootprint(h.id)
      await load()
      Taro.showToast({ title: '多了一个脚印', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '打卡失败，请重试', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleRule(h: Branch, rule: 'daily' | 'weekly') {
    try {
      await updateBranchRule(h.id, rule)
      await load()
      Taro.showToast({ title: '规则已更新', icon: 'success' })
      setEditId(null)
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleArchive(h: Branch) {
    try {
      await archiveBranch(h.id)
      await load()
      Taro.showToast({ title: '已归档', icon: 'success' })
      setEditId(null)
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleCreate() {
    if (!newName.trim()) {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '给习惯起个名字', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    try {
      // 习惯支线挂在当前第一条主线下（演示数据结构）
      const { getMainlines } = await import('@/db/api')
      const lines = await getMainlines()
      if (lines.length === 0) {
        Taro.showToast({ title: '先创建一条主线', icon: 'none' })
        return
      }
      await createBranches(lines[0].id, [{ name: newName.trim(), type: 'habit', repeat_rule: newRule }])
      setNewName('')
      setCreating(false)
      await load()
      Taro.showToast({ title: '习惯支线已建好', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '创建失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  // 本周脚印统计
  const weekStats = useMemo(() => {
    const stats: Record<string, number> = {}
    const now = new Date()
    const ws = new Date(now)
    ws.setDate(now.getDate() - ((now.getDay() + 6) % 7))
    const wsStr = `${ws.getFullYear()}-${String(ws.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(ws.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`
    habits.forEach((h) => {
      stats[h.name] = (footMap[h.id] ?? []).filter((d) => d >= wsStr).length
    })
    return stats
  }, [habits, footMap])

  // 当月日历
  const calendar = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date()
    const year = now.getFullYear()
    const month = now.getMonth()
    const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1)
    const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate()
    const lead = (firstDay.getDay() + 6) % 7
    const cells: (string | null)[] = Array.from({ length: lead }, () => null)
    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
      cells.push(`${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`)
    }
    return cells
  }, [])

  function hasFoot(date: string): boolean {
    return habits.some((h) => (footMap[h.id] ?? []).includes(date))
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在数脚印…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col px-4 py-4 gap-5">
      {/* 习惯列表 */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="text-2xl font-hand text-foreground">习惯支线</p>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 flex items-center gap-1" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
            <div className="i-lucide-plus w-5 h-5 text-primary-foreground" />
            <span className="text-lg text-primary-foreground">新建</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {creating && (
          <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-primary shadow-sketch-sm p-4 flex flex-col gap-3 animate-sketch-in">
            <div className="rounded-sketch border-2 border-input bg-background px-3 py-2 overflow-hidden">
              <input
                className="w-full text-xl text-foreground bg-transparent outline-none"
                placeholder="例如：每日背单词"
                value={newName}
                onInput={(e) => { const ev = e as any; setNewName(ev.detail?.value ?? ev.target?.value ?? '') }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {([['daily', '每日'], ['weekly', '每周']] as const).map(([k, label]) => (
                <button key={k} type="button" className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-1.5 ${newRule === k ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-background border-border/40'}`} onClick={() => setNewRule(k)}>
                  <span className={`text-lg ${newRule === k ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{label}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button type="button" className="flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary" onClick={handleCreate}>
                <div className="py-2 text-lg text-primary-foreground">建好</div>
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-border/40" onClick={() => setCreating(false)}>
                <div className="py-2 text-lg text-foreground">取消</div>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {habits.length === 0 && !creating && (
          <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-dashed border-border/50 p-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div className="i-lucide-footprints w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground" />
            <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">还没有习惯支线，建一条慢慢走</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {habits.map((h) => {
          const feet = footMap[h.id] ?? []
          const doneToday = feet.includes(today)
          return (
            <div key={h.id} className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 shadow-sketch-sm p-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="i-lucide-footprints w-6 h-6 text-primary" />
                  <span className="text-xl text-foreground">{h.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{h.repeat_rule === 'daily' ? '每日' : '每周'}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{feet.length} 个脚印</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={`press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 px-4 py-1.5 ${doneToday ? 'bg-muted border-border/40' : 'bg-primary border-primary shadow-sketch-sm'}`}
                    onClick={() => handleCheckin(h)}
                  >
                    <span className={`text-lg ${doneToday ? 'text-muted-foreground' : 'text-primary-foreground'}`}>{doneToday ? '今天走过' : '打卡'}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">本周 {weekStats[h.name] ?? 0} 个脚印</span>
                <button type="button" className="i-lucide-settings-2 w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" onClick={() => setEditId(editId === h.id ? null : h.id)} />
              </div>
              {editId === h.id && (
                <div className="rounded-sketch bg-muted p-3 flex flex-col gap-2 animate-sketch-in">
                  <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">重复规则</p>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    {([['daily', '每日'], ['weekly', '每周']] as const).map(([k, label]) => (
                      <button key={k} type="button" className={`flex-1 press-sketch rounded-sketch border-2 py-1.5 ${h.repeat_rule === k ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card border-border/40'}`} onClick={() => handleRule(h, k)}>
                        <span className={`text-lg ${h.repeat_rule === k ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{label}</span>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/40" onClick={() => handleArchive(h)}>
                    <div className="py-1.5 text-lg text-muted-foreground">归档这条习惯</div>
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      {/* 日历视图：只标有脚印的日子 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-card border-2 border-border/30 p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">这个月的脚印</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-2">
          {['一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六', '日'].map((w) => (
            <span key={w} className="text-base text-muted-foreground text-center">{w}</span>
          ))}
          {calendar.map((d, i) => {
            if (!d) return <span key={`empty-${i}`} />
            const day = Number(d.slice(8))
            const has = hasFoot(d)
            const isToday = d === today
            return (
              <div key={d} className={`w-full aspect-square rounded-sketch-sm flex items-center justify-center ${has ? 'bg-primary' : 'bg-transparent'} ${isToday ? 'border-2 border-accent' : ''}`}>
                <span className={`text-base ${has ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`}>{day}</span>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
        <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">空着的日子就是没走的日子，不用补，也不提醒。</p>
      </div>

      {/* 本周脚印统计 */}
      <div className="rounded-sketch bg-muted border-2 border-dashed border-border/50 p-4 flex flex-col gap-2 mb-6">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">本周脚印</p>
        {habits.length === 0 && <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">还没有习惯</p>}
        {habits.map((h) => (
          <div key={h.id} className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-lg text-foreground">{h.name}</span>
            <span className="text-lg text-primary">{weekStats[h.name] ?? 0}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

**FileEditAction**
